(kauśikasūtra 1,1.1) atha vidhiṃ vakṣyāmaḥ

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.2) sa punarāmnāyapratyayaḥ

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.3) āmnāyaḥ punarmantrāśca brāhmaṇāni ca

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.4) tadyathābrāhmaṇaṃ vidhirevaṃ karmaliṅgā mantrāḥ

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.5) tathānyārthaḥ

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.6) tathā brāhmaṇaliṅgā mantrāḥ

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.7) tadabhāve saṃpradāyaḥ

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.8) pramuktatvādbrāhmaṇānām

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.9) yajñaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.10) devānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ ca

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.11) prāṅmukha upāṃśu karoti

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.12) yajñopavītī devānām

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.13) prācīnavītī pitṝṇām

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.14) prāgudagvā devānām

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.15) dakṣiṇā pitṝṇām

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.16) prāgudagapavargaṃ devānām

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.17) dakṣiṇāpratyagapavargaṃ pitṝṇām

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.18) sakṛtkarma pitṝṇām

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.19) tryavarārdhaṃ devānām

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.20) yathādiṣṭaṃ vā

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.21) abhidakṣiṇamācāro devānām

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.22) prasavyaṃ pitṝṇām

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.23) svāhākāravaṣṭkārapradānā devāḥ

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.24) svadhākāranamaskārapradānā pitaraḥ

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.25) upamūlalūnaṃ barhiḥ pitṝṇām

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.26) parvasu devānām

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.27) <pra yacha parśum [12.3.31 ]> iti darbhāhārāya dātraṃ prayachati

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.28) <oṣadhīrdāntu parvan [12.3.31 part]> iti_upari parvaṇāṃ lūtvā tūṣṇīmāhṛtya_uttarato_agnerupasādayati

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.29) nāgniṃ viparyāvarteta

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.30) nāntarā yajñāṅgāni vyaveyāt

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.31) dakṣiṇaṃ jānu prabhujya juhoti

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.32) yā pūrvā paurṇamāsī sānumatiryā_uttarā sā rākā[dṛṣṭavyam‌  cf. 7.11.2]

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.33) yā pūrvāmāvāsyā sā sinīvālī yā_uttarā sā kuhūḥ [dṛṣṭavyam‌  aitareyabrāhmaṇa 7.11.2]

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.34) adya_upavasatha iti_upavatsyadbhaktamaśnāti

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.35) madhulavaṇamāṃsamāṣavarjam

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.36) <mamāgne varcaḥ [5.3.1]> iti samidha ādhāya vratamupaiti

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.37) <vratena tvāṃ vratapate [7.74.4]> iti vā

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.38) brahmacārī vratī

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.39) adhaḥ śayīta

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.40) prātarhute_agnau <karmaṇe vāṃ veṣāya vāṃ sukṛtāya vām [dṛṣṭavyam‌  ṭṣ1.1.4.1 ityādi, Kauśṣ58.5]> iti pāṇī prakṣālyāpareṇāgnerdarbhānāstīrya teṣūttaramānaḍuhaṃ rohitaṃ carma prāggrīvamuttaraloma prastīrya pavitre kurute

(kauśikasūtra 1,1.41) darbhau_aprachinnāntau prakṣālyānulomamanumārṣṭi <viṣṇormanasā pūte sthaḥ [ড়ṣ20.45.6, ṃṣ4.1.57.15 ityādi]> iti



(kauśikasūtra 1,2.1) <tvaṃ bhūmimatyeṣyojasā tvaṃ vedyāṃ sīdasi cāruradhvare | tvāṃ pavitramṛṣayo bharantastvaṃ punīhi duritānyasmat || [ড়ṣ11.13.3 = ̭ṣṣ19.33.3 (butsakalapāṭha!)]> iti pavitre antardhāya havirnirvapati <devasya tvā savituḥ prasave_aśvinorbāhubhyāṃ pūṣṇo hastābhyāṃ juṣṭaṃ nirvapāmi [sakala  dṛṣṭavyam‌  19.51.2, ড়ṣ5.40.1, ṭṣ1.1.4.2 ityādi]> iti

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.2) evam <agniṣomābhyām [ibid.]> iti

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.3) <indrāgnibhyām []> iti_amāvāsyāyām

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.4) nityaṃ pūrvamāgneyam

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.5) niruptaṃ pavitrābhyāṃ prokṣati <amuṣmai tvā juṣṭam []> yathādevatam

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.6) ulūkhalamusalaṃ śūrpaṃ prakṣālitaṃ carmaṇi_ādhāya vrīhīnulūkhala opyāvaghnaṃstrirhaviṣkṛtā vācaṃ visṛjati <haviṣkṛdā dravehi [dṛṣṭavyam‌  āap̭ṣṣ1.19.9 ityādi]> iti

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.7) +avahatya [ed. apahatya] suphalīkṛtān kṛtvā triḥ prakṣālya taṇḍulān <agne varuryajñiyastvādhi arukṣat[11.1.16]> iti carumadhidadhāti

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.8) <śuddhāḥ pūtāḥ [11.1.17]> iti_udakamāsiñcati

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.9) <brahmaṇā śuddhāḥ [11.1.18]> iti taṇḍulān

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.10) <pari tvāgne puraṃ vayam [7.71.1]> iti triḥ paryagni karoti

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.11) nekṣaṇena triḥ pradakṣiṇamudāyauti

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.12) ata ūrdhvaṃ yathākāmam

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.13) uttarato_agnerupasādayatīdhmam

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.14) uttaraṃ barhiḥ

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.15) <agnaye tvā juṣṭaṃ prokṣāmi [i.a. Kṣ1.105.22]> itīdhmam

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.16) <pṛthivyai []> iti barhiḥ

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.17) darbhamuṣṭimabhyukṣya paścādagneḥ prāgagraṃ nidadhāti <ūrṇamradaṃ prathasva svāsasthaṃ devebhyaḥ [corruptdṛṣṭavyam‌  i.a. Kṣ1.116.23]> iti

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.18) darbhāṇāmapādāya <ṛṣīṇāṃ prastaro_asi [16.2.6]> iti dakṣiṇato_agnerbrahmāsanaṃ nidadhāti

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.19) purastādagnerāstīrya teṣāṃ mūlāni_apareṣāṃ prāntairavachādayan parisarpati dakṣiṇenāgnimā paścārdhāt

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.20) <pari stṛṇīhi [7.99.1]> iti saṃpreṣyati

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.21) <devasya tvā savituḥ prasave_aśvinorbāhubhyāṃ pūṣṇo hastābhyāṃ prasūtaḥ praśiṣā paristṛṇāmi []> iti

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.22) evamuttarato_ayujo dhātūn kurvan

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.23) yatra samāgachanti taddakṣiṇottaraṃ kṛṇoti

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.24) stīrṇaṃ prokṣati <haviṣāṃ tvā juṣṭaṃ prokṣāmi []> iti

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.25) nānabhyukṣitaṃ saṃstīrṇamupayogaṃ labheta

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.26) naidho_abhyādānam

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.27) nānutpūtaṃ haviḥ

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.28) nāprokṣitaṃ yajñāṅgam

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.29) tasmin prakṣālitopavātāni nidadhāti

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.30) sruvamājyadhānīṃ ca

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.31) vilīnapūtamājyaṃ gṛhītvādhiśritya paryagni kṛtvā_udagudvāsya paścādagnerupasādya_udagagrābhyāṃ pavitrābhyāmutpunāti

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.32) <viṣṇormanasā pūtamasi [ড়ṣ20.45.7 ]>

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.33) <devastvā savitā_utpunātu [ড়ṣ20.45.7 ]>

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.34) <achidreṇa tvā pavitreṇa śatadhāreṇa sahasradhāreṇa supvotpunāmi [ড়ṣ20.45.7 d]> iti tṛtīyam

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.35) tūṣṇīṃ caturtham

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.36) śṛtaṃ havirabhighārayati <madhvā samañjan ghṛtavatkarātha [dṛṣṭavyam‌  ড়ṣ5.16.3 ]> iti

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.37) abhighārya_udañcamudvāsayati <udvāsayāgneḥ śṛtamakarma havyamā sīda pṛṣṭhamamṛtasya dhāma [dṛṣṭavyam‌  ড়ṣ5.16.3 b]> iti

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.38) paścādājyasya nidhāyālaṃkṛtya samānena_utpunāti

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.39) <adārasṛt[1.20.1]> iti_avekṣate

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.40) <uttiṣṭhata [7.72.13]> iti_aindram

(kauśikasūtra 1,2.41) <agnirbhūyām [12.1.1921]> iti tisṛbhirupasamādadhāti <asmai kṣatrāṇi [7.78.2]> <etamidhmam [10.6.35]> iti vā



(kauśikasūtra 1,3.1) <yunajmi tvā brahmaṇā daivyena havyāyāsmai voḍheva [ed. voḍhave misprint. Cf. Zehnder1999 191. ṃss. voḍheva] jātavedaḥ | indhānāstvā suprajasaḥ suvīrā jyogjīvema balihṛto vayaṃ te [ṃṣ1.4.147.6 ityādi]> iti

(kauśikasūtra 1,3.2) dakṣiṇato jāṅmāyanamudapātramupasādyābhimantrayate <tathodapātraṃ dhāraya yathāgre brahmaṇaspatiḥ | satyadharmā adīdharaddevasya savituḥ save []> iti

(kauśikasūtra 1,3.3) atha_udakamāsiñcati <iheta devīramṛtaṃ vasānā hiraṇyavarṇā anavadyarūpāḥ | āpaḥ samudro varuṇaśca rājā saṃpātabhāgān haviṣo juṣantām [, dṛṣṭavyam‌  ড়ṣ2.40.1 ] || indrapraśiṣṭā varuṇaprasūtā apaḥ samudrāddivamudvahantu | indrapraśiṣṭā varuṇaprasūtā divaspṛthivyā śriyamudvahantu [, dṛṣṭavyam‌  4.27.4 , ṛV 10.66.2 ]> iti

(kauśikasūtra 1,3.4) <ṛtaṃ tvā satyena pariṣiñcāmi jātavedaḥ [dṛṣṭavyam‌  Vaitṣ7.4, ṭB 2.1.11.1 ityādi]> iti saha havirbhiḥ paryukṣya jīvābhir<[19.69.14]> ācamya_utthāya vedaprapadbhiḥ prapadyate_<o̭ṃ prapadye bhūḥ prapadye bhuvaḥ prapadye svaḥ prapadye janatprapadye [ṭā 4.42.24]> iti

(kauśikasūtra 1,3.5) prapadya paścātstīrṇasya darbhānāstīrya <ahe daidhiṣavyodatastiṣṭhānyasya sadane sīda yo'smatpākataraḥ [ṭṣ3.2.4.4, Vaitṣ1.20]>_iti brahmāsanamanvīkṣate

(kauśikasūtra 1,3.6) <nirastaḥ parāgvasuḥ saha pāpmanā nirastaḥ so'stu yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ ca vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ [ṅobhṅṣ1.6.14, ̭ṣBṃ 1.5.1.23 ityādi]>_iti dakṣiṇā tṛṇaṃ nirasyati (dṛṣṭavyam‌  139.38 f.)

(kauśikasūtra 1,3.7) tadanvālabhya japati <idamahamarvāgvasoḥ sadane sīdāmi_[ṅB 2.1.1, ̭ṣBṃ 1.5.1.24]_ṛtasya sadane sīdāmi satyasya sadane sīdāmi_[1.2.2 ityādi]_iṣṭasya sadane sīdāmi pūrtasya sadane sīdāmi [] māmṛṣaddeva [em. Bloomfieldṅṅā 1902 514  ed. eva] barhiḥ svāsasthaṃ tvādhyāsadeyamūrṇamradamanabhiśokam [dṛṣṭavyam‌  āāśv̭ṣṣ1.4.7]>

(kauśikasūtra 1,3.8) <vimṛgvarīṃ [12.1.29]>_ityupaviśyāsanīyaṃ brahmajapaṃ japati <bṛhaspatirbrahmā brahmasadana āsiṣyate (thusBl.) [āāśv̭ṣṣ1.12.9]> <bṛhaspate yajñaṃ gopāya [āāśv̭ṣṣ1.12.9, ṭB 3.7.6.3]> <yadududvata unnivataḥ śakeyam [ṭṣ3.2.4.4]> iti

(kauśikasūtra 1,3.9) darbhaiḥ sruvaṃ nirmṛjya <niṣṭaptaṃ rakṣo niṣṭaptā arātayaḥ pratyuṣṭaṃ rakṣaḥ pratyuṣṭā arātayaḥ [ṭṣ1.1.2.1, ṭB 3.7.6.3]> iti pratapya mūle sruvaṃ gṛhītvā japati <viṣṇorhasto_asi dakṣiṇaḥ pūṣṇā datto bṛhaspateḥ | taṃ tvāhaṃ sruvamā dade devānāṃ havyavāhanam [ṣankhṅṣ1.9.3] || ayaṃ sruvo vi dadhāti homān_śatākṣarachandasā jāgatena | sarvā yajñasya samanakti viṣṭhā bārhaspatyā_iṣṭiḥ śarmaṇā daivyena [āpṣṣ4.7.2]> iti

(kauśikasūtra 1,3.10) <oṃ bhūḥ śaṃ bhūtyai tvā gṛhṇe bhūtaye []> iti prathamaṃ grahaṃ gṛhṇāti

(kauśikasūtra 1,3.11) <oṃ bhuvaḥ śaṃ puṣṭyai tvā gṛhṇe puṣṭaye []> iti dvitīyam

(kauśikasūtra 1,3.12) <oṃ svaḥ śaṃ tvā gṛhṇe sahasrapoṣāya []> iti tṛtīyam

(kauśikasūtra 1,3.13) <oṃ jagat_śaṃ tvā gṛhṇe_aparimitapoṣāya []> iti caturtham

(kauśikasūtra 1,3.14) rājakarmābhicārikeṣu <amuṣya tvā prāṇāya gṛhṇe_apānāya vyānāya samānāya_udānāya []> iti pañcamam

(kauśikasūtra 1,3.15) <agnāvagniḥ hṛdā pūtam [4.39.910]> <purastādyuktaḥ [5.29.1]> <yajñasya cakṣuḥ [2.35.5 = 19.58.5]> iti juhoti

(kauśikasūtra 1,3.16) paścādagnermadhyadeśe samānatra purastāddhomān

(kauśikasūtra 1,3.17) dakṣiṇenāgnimudapātra ājyāhutīnāṃ saṃpātānānayati

(kauśikasūtra 1,3.18) purastāddhoma ājyabhāgaḥ saṃsthitahomaḥ samṛddhiḥ śāntānām

(kauśikasūtra 1,3.19) iti_etau_ājyabhāgau



(kauśikasūtra 1,4.1)  <vṛṣṇe bṛhate svarvide agnaye śulkaṃ harāmi tviṣīmate | sa na sthirān balavataḥ kṛṇotu jyokca no jīvātave dadhāti || [ড়ṣ19.52.7]> <agnaye svāhā> iti_uttarapūrvārdhe_āgneyamājyabhāgaṃ juhoti

(kauśikasūtra 1,4.2) dakṣiṇapūrvārdhe somāya <tvaṃ soma divyo nṛcakṣāḥ sugā̭masmabhyaṃ patho anu khyaḥ | abhi no gotraṃ viduṣa iva neṣo'chā no vācamuśatīṃ jigāsi || [ড়ṣ1.51.3]> <somāya svāhā> iti

(kauśikasūtra 1,4.3) madhye haviḥ

(kauśikasūtra 1,4.4) upastīryājyaṃ saṃhatābhyāmaṅgulibhyāṃ dvirhaviṣo_avadyati madhyātpūrvārdhāt_ca

(kauśikasūtra 1,4.5) avattamabhighārya dvirhaviḥ pratyabhighārayati

(kauśikasūtra 1,4.6) yato yato_avadyati tadanupūrvam

(kauśikasūtra 1,4.7) evaṃ sarvāṇi_avadānāni

(kauśikasūtra 1,4.8) anyatra sauviṣṭakṛtāt

(kauśikasūtra 1,4.9) <udenamuttaraṃ naya [6.5.1]> iti purastāddhomasaṃhatāṃ pūrvām

(kauśikasūtra 1,4.10) evaṃ pūrvāṃpūrvāṃ saṃhatāṃ juhoti

(kauśikasūtra 1,4.11) svāhāntābhiḥ pratyṛcaṃ homāḥ

(kauśikasūtra 1,4.12) yāmuttarāmagnerājyabhāgasya juhoti rakṣodevatyā sā yāṃ dakṣiṇataḥ somasya pitṛdevatyā sā

(kauśikasūtra 1,4.13) tasmādantarā hotavyā devaloke_eva hūyante
(kauśikasūtra 1,4.14) yāṃ hutvā pūrvāmaparāṃ juhoti sāpakramantī sa pāpīyān yajamāno bhavati

(kauśikasūtra 1,4.15) yāṃ parāṃparāṃ saṃhatāṃ juhoti sābhikramantī sa vasīyān yajamāno bhavati

(kauśikasūtra 1,4.16) yāmanagnau juhoti sāndhā tayā cakṣuryajamānasya mīyate so_andhaṃbhāvuko yajamāno bhavati

(kauśikasūtra 1,4.17) yāṃ dhūme juhoti sā tamasi hūyate so_arocako yajamāno bhavati

(kauśikasūtra 1,4.18) yāṃ jyotiṣmati juhoti tayā brahmavarcasī bhavati tasmāt_jyotiṣmati hotavyam

(kauśikasūtra 1,4.19) evam <asmai kṣatramagnīṣomau [6.54.2]> iti_agniṣomīyasya



(kauśikasūtra 1,5.1) <agnīṣomā savedasā sahūtī vanataṃ giraḥ | sa devatrā babhūvathuḥ || yuvametāni divi rocanānyagniśca soma sakratū adhattam | yuvaṃ sindhṷ̄mrabhiśasteravadyādagnīṣomāvamuñcataṃ gṛbhītān || agnīśomā ya āhutiṃ yo vāṃ dāśāddhaviśkṛtim | sa prajayā suvīryaṃ viśvamāyurvyaśnavat || [ṛV 1.93.9,5,3 ityādi]>

(kauśikasūtra 1,5.2) <indrāgnī rocanā divaḥ pari vājeṣu bhūṣathaḥ | tadvāṃ ceti pra vīryam || [ṛV 3.12.9]> <śnathadvṛtramuta sanoti vājamindrā yo agnī sahurī saparyāt | irajyantā vasavyasya bhūreḥ sahastamā sahasā vājayantā || [ṛV 6.60.1]> <indrāgnī asmān rakṣatāṃ yau prajānāṃ prajāvatī | sa prajayā suvīryaṃ viśvamāyurvyaśnavat || [10.1.21 d+ ṛV 1.93.3 d]> <gomaddhiraṇyavadvasu yadvāmaśvāvadīmahe | indrāgnī vanemahi || [ṛV 7.94.9]> <svāhā> iti

(kauśikasūtra 1,5.3) aindrāgnasya haviṣo_amāvāsyāyām

(kauśikasūtra 1,5.4) prāksviṣṭakṛtaḥ pārvaṇahomau samṛddhihomāḥ kāmyahomāśca

(kauśikasūtra 3,1[18].1) pūrvasya pūrvasyāṃ paurṇamāsyāmastamita udakānte kṛṣṇacailaparihito nirṛtikarmāṇi prayuṅkte

(kauśikasūtra 3,1[18].12) savyaṃ praharatyupānahau ca

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].5) kṣīraṃ cājyaṃ ca sārasvato madhuparkaḥ

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].6) surā cājyaṃ ca mausalo madhuparkaḥ

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].7) sa khalu_eṣa dvaye bhavati sautrāmaṇyāṃ ca rājasūye ca

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].8) udakaṃ  cājyaṃ ca vāruṇo madhuparkaḥ

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].9) tailaṃ cājyaṃ ca śrāvaṇo madhuparkaḥ

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].10) tailaśca piṇḍaśca pārivrājako madhuparkaḥ

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].11) iti khalu_eṣa navavidho madhuparko bhavati

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].12) athāsmai gāṃ vedayante gaurbho iti

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].13) tān pratimantrayate | <bhūtamasi bhavadasyannaṃ prāṇo bahurbhava | jyeṣṭhaṃ yannāma nāmata oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svarjanadom []> iti

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].14) atisṛjati | <mātādityānāṃ duhitā vasūnāṃ svasā rudrāṇāmamṛtasya nābhiḥ | pra ṇo vocaṃ cikituṣe janāya mā gāmanāgāmaditiṃ vadhiṣṭa || [dṛṣṭavyam‌  .ṛV 8.101.15]> oṃ tṛṇāni gaurattviti āha

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].15) <sūyavasād[7.73.11]> iti pratiṣṭhamānāmanumantrayate

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].16) nālohito madhuparko bhavati

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].17) nānujñānamadhīmahe_iti kuruta_ityeva brūyāt

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].18) <svadhite mainaṃ hiṃsīḥ [Vṣṃ 6.15, ṃnB 1.6.6]>_iti śastraṃ prayachati

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].19) pāpmānaṃ me'pa jahīti kartāramanumantrayate

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].20) āgneyīṃ vapāṃ kuryuḥ

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].21) api vā brāhmaṇa eva prāśnīyāttaddevataṃ hi taddhavirbhavati

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].22) athāsmai snānamanulepanaṃ mālābhyañjanamiti

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].23) yadatra_upasamāhāryaṃ bhavati tadupasamāhṛtya

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].24) atha_upāsakāḥ prāya_upāsakāḥ smo bho iti vedayante

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].25) tān pratimantrayate | <bhūyāṃso bhūyāsma ye ca no bhūyasaḥ kārṣṭāpi ca no'nye bhūyāṃso jāyantām>

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].26) asya ca dāturiti dātāramīkṣate

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].27) athānnāhārāḥ prāpyānnāhārāḥ smo bho iti vedayante

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].28) tān pratimantrayate | <annādā bhūyāsma ye ca no'nādān kārṣṭāpi ca no'nye'nnādā bhūyāṃso jāyantām>

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].29) asya ca dāturiti dātāramīkṣate

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].30) āhṛte_anne juhoti <yatkāma kāmayamānā [19.52.1, sakala followsthusড়ṣ1.30.5]> ityetayā

(kauśikasūtra 12,3[92].32) eṣa ācāryakalpa eṣa ṛtvikkalpa eṣa saṃyuktakalpa eṣa vivāhakalpa eṣo_atithikalpa eṣo'tithikalpaḥ

(kauśikasūtra 13,2[94].1) atha yatra_etāni varṣāṇi varṣanti ghṛtaṃ māṃsaṃ madhu ca yaddhiraṇyaṃ yāni cāpi_anyāni ghorāṇi varṣāṇi varṣanti tatparābhavati kulaṃ vā grāmo vā janapado vā

(kauśikasūtra 13,2[94].2) tatra rājā bhūmipatirvidvāṃsaṃ brahmāṇamichet

(kauśikasūtra 13,2[94].3) eṣa ha vai vidvān yadbhṛgvaṅgirovit

(kauśikasūtra 13,2[94].4) ete ha vā asya sarvasya śamayitāraḥ pālayitāro yadbhṛgvaṅgirasaḥ

(kauśikasūtra 13,2[94].5) sa āha_upakalpayadhvamiti

(kauśikasūtra 13,2[94].6) tadupakalpayante kaṃsamahate vasane śuddhamājyaṃ śāntā oṣadhīrnavamudakumbham

(kauśikasūtra 13,2[94].7) trīṇi parvāṇi karmaṇaḥ paurṇamāsyamāvāsye puṇyaṃ nakṣatram

(kauśikasūtra 13,2[94].8) api cedeva yadā kadā ārtāya kuryāt

(kauśikasūtra 13,2[94].9) snāto_ahatavasanaḥ surabhirvratavān karmaṇya upavasati_ekarātraṃ trirātraṃ ṣaḍrātraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ vā

(kauśikasūtra 13,2[94].10) dvādaśyāḥ prātaryatra_evādaḥ patitaṃ bhavati tata uttaramagnimupasamādhāya

(kauśikasūtra 13,2[94].11) parisamuhya paryukṣya paristīrya barhirudapātramupasādya paricaraṇenājyaṃ paricarya

(kauśikasūtra 13,2[94].12) nityān purastāddhomān hutvājyabhāgau ca

(kauśikasūtra 13,2[94].13) atha juhoti

(kauśikasūtra 13,2[94].14) <ghṛtasya dhārā iha yā varṣanti pakvaṃ māṃsaṃ madhu ca yaddhiraṇyam | dviṣantametā anu yantu vṛṣṭayo'pāṃ vṛṣṭayo bahulāḥ santu mahyam || lohitavarṣaṃ madhupāṃsuvarṣaṃ yadvā varṣaṃ ghoramaniṣṭamanyat | dviṣantamete anu yantu sarve parāñco yantu nirvartamānāḥ || agnaye svāhā []>_iti hutvā

(kauśikasūtra 13,2[94].18) sā tatra prāyaścittiḥ

(kauśikasūtra 13,6[98].2) <acyutā dyauracyutamantarikṣamacyutā bhūmirdiśo acyutā imāḥ | acyuto'yaṃ rodhāvarodhāddhruvo rāṣṭre prati tiṣṭhāmi jiṣṇuḥ || yathā sūryo divi rocate yathāntarikṣaṃ mātariśvābhivaste | yathāgniḥ pṛthivīmā viveśaivāyaṃ dhruvo acyuto astu jiṣṇuḥ || yathā devo divi stanayan vi rājati yathā varṣaṃ varṣakāmāya varṣati | yathāpaḥ pṛthivīmā viviśurevāyaṃ dhruvo acyuto astu jiṣṇuḥ || yathā purīṣaṃ nadyaḥ samudramahorātre apramādaṃ kṣaranti | evā viśaḥ saṃmanaso havaṃ me'pramādamihopa yantu sarvāḥ || dṛṃhatāṃ devī saha devatābhirdhruvā dṛḍhācyutā me astu bhūmiḥ | sarvapāpmānamapanudyāsmadamitrānme dviṣato'nu vidhyatu || pṛthivyai svāhā [dṛṣṭavyam‌  āVড়riś11.1.11]>_iti hutvā

(kauśikasūtra 13,14[106].5) parisamuhya paryukṣya paristīrya barhiḥ śamyāḥ paridhīn kṛtvā

(kauśikasūtra 13,14[106].6) atha juhoti | <vittirasi puṣṭirasi śrīrasi prājāpatyānāṃ tāṃ tvāhaṃ mayi puṣṭikāmo juhomi svāhā []>

(kauśikasūtra 13,14[106].7) <kumudvatī puṣkariṇī sītā sarvāṅgaśobhanī | kṛṣiḥ sahasraprakārā pratyaṣṭā śrīriyaṃ mayi || urvīṃ tvāhurmanuṣyāḥ śriyaṃ tvā manaso viduḥ | āśaye'nnasya no dhehyanamīvasya śuṣmiṇaḥ || [, dṛṣṭavyam‌  ̭ṣāṅkhṅṣ1.27.7 ityādi]>  <parjanyapatni hariṇyabhijitāsyabhi no vada | kālanetre haviṣo no juṣasva tṛptiṃ no dhehi dvipade catuṣpade || yābhirdevā asurānakalpayan yātūnmanūn gandharvān rākṣasāṃśca | tābhirno adya sumanā upā gahi sahasrāpoṣaṃ subhage rarāṇā || [, dṛṣṭavyam‌  7.48.2 d]>  <hiraṇyasrakpuṣkariṇī śyāmā sarvāṅgaśobhanī | kṛṣirhiraṇyaprakārā pratyaṣṭā śrīriyaṃ mayi || aśvibhyāṃ devi saha saṃvidānā indreṇa rādhena saha puṣṭyā na ā gahi | viśastvā rāsantāṃ pradiśo'nu sarvā ahorātrārdhamāsamāsā ārtavā ṛtubhiḥ saha || bhartrī devānāmuta martyānāṃ bhartrī prajānāmuta mānuṣāṇām | hastibhiritarāsaiḥ kṣetrasārathibhiḥ saha | hiraṇyairaśvairā gobhiḥ pratyaṣṭā śrīriyaṃ mayi || []>

(kauśikasūtra 13,14[106].8) atra śūnāsīrāṇi_anuyojayet

(kauśikasūtra 13,14[106].9) varamanaḍvāhamiti samānam



(kauśikasūtra 13,15[107].1) atha yatra_etatsṛjantyorvā kṛtantyorvā nānā tantū saṃsṛjato <manāyai tantuṃ prathamaṃ [ড়ṣ2.87.1]>_ityetena sūktena juhuyāt

(kauśikasūtra 13,15[107].2) <manāyai tantuṃ prathamaṃ paśyedanyā atanvata | tannārīḥ pra bravīmi vaḥ sādhvīrvaḥ santūrvarīḥ || sādhurvastanturbhavatu sādhuretu ratho vṛtaḥ | atho horvarīryūyaṃ prātarvoḍhave dhāvata || khargalā iva patvarīrapāmugramivāyanam | patantu patvarīrivorvarīḥ sādhunā pathā || apācyau te totudyete todenāśvatarāviva | pra stomamurvarīṇāṃ śaśayānāmastāviṣam || nārī pañcamayūkhaṃ sūtravatkṛṇute vasu | ariṣṭo asya vastā prendra vāsa utodira || [ড়ṣ2.87]>

(kauśikasūtra 13,15[107].3) vāsaḥ kartre dadyāt

(kauśikasūtra 13,15[107].4) sā tatra prāyaścittiḥ



(kauśikasūtra 13,16[108].1) atha yatra_etadagnināgniḥ saṃsṛjyate <bhavataṃ naḥ samanasau samokasāv[]> ityetena sūktena juhuyāt

(kauśikasūtra 13,16[108].2) <bhavataṃ naḥ samanasau samokasāvarepasau | mā hi̭msiṣṭaṃ yajñapatiṃ mā yajñaṃ jātavedasau śivau bhavatamadya naḥ [dṛṣṭavyam‌  Vṣṃ 5.3 ityādi]> <agnināgniḥ saṃsṛjyase kavirbṛhaspatiryuvā | havyavāṅjuhvāsyaḥ || [dṛṣṭavyam‌  .ṛV 1.12.6 ityādi]> <tvaṃ hyagne agninā vipro vipreṇa san satā | sakhā sakhyā samidhyase || [dṛṣṭavyam‌  .ṛV 8.43.14]> <pāhi no agna ekayā pāhi na uta dvitīyayā | pāhi gīrbhistisṛbhirūrjāṃpate pāhi catasṛbhirvaso || samīcī māhanī pātāmāyuṣmatyā ṛco mā satsi | tanūpātsāmno vasuvidaṃ lokamanusaṃcarāṇi || [ড়ṣ20.52.45]>

(kauśikasūtra 13,16[108].3) rukmaṃ kartre dadyāt

(kauśikasūtra 13,16[108].4) sā tatra prāyaścittiḥ



(kauśikasūtra 13,17[109].1) atha yatra_etadayamasūryamau janayati tāṃ śāntyudakenābhyukṣya dohayitvā

(kauśikasūtra 13,17[109].5) <ekaikayaiṣā sṛṣṭyā saṃ babhūva [3.28.1]> ityetena sūktenājyaṃ juhvan

(kauśikasūtra 13,17[109].6) udapātre saṃpātānānayati

(kauśikasūtra 13,17[109].7) uttamaṃ saṃpātamodane pratyānayati

(kauśikasūtra 13,17[109].10) sā tatra prāyaścittiḥ

(kauśikasūtra 13,18[110].2) evaṃ paristīrya

(kauśikasūtra 13,18[110].3) evamupasādya

(kauśikasūtra 13,18[110].4) etena_eva sūktenājyaṃ juhvan

(kauśikasūtra 13,18[110].6) udapātrādenānācāmayati ca saṃprokṣati ca

(kauśikasūtra 13,18[110].7) tāṃ tasya_eva dadyāt

(kauśikasūtra 13,18[110].8) sā tatra prāyaścittiḥ



(kauśikasūtra 13,19[111].1) atha ced_mānuṣī syādevameva prāñcamidhmamupasamādhāya

(kauśikasūtra 13,19[111].2) evaṃ paristīrya

(kauśikasūtra 13,19[111].3) evamupasādya

(kauśikasūtra 13,19[111].4) upasthe jātakau_ādhāya

(kauśikasūtra 13,19[111].5) etena_eva sūktenājyaṃ juhvan

(kauśikasūtra 13,19[111].9) tāṃ tasya_eva dadyāt

(kauśikasūtra 13,20[112].1) atha yatra_etaddhenavo lohitaṃ duhate <yaḥ pauruṣeyeṇa kraviṣā samaṅkte [8.3.15]> ityetābhiścatasṛbhirjuhuyāt

(kauśikasūtra 13,20[112].2) varāṃ dhenuṃ kartre dadyāt

(kauśikasūtra 13,20[112].3) sā tatra prāyaścittiḥ

(kauśikasūtra 13,21[113].3) <mā no vidan [1.19.1]> <namo devavadhebhyo [6.13.1]>_iti etābhyāṃ sūktābhyāṃ juhuyāt

(kauśikasūtra 13,21[113].4) sā tatra prāyaścittiḥ

(kauśikasūtra 13,22[114].2) <yogakṣemaṃ dhenuṃ vājapatnīmindrāgnibhyāṃ preṣite jañjabhāne | tasmānmāmagne pari pāhi ghorātpra +ṇo jāyantāṃ mithunāni rūpaśaḥ || indrāgnibhyāṃ svāhā [, dṛṣṭavyam‌  Kauśṣ128.2]>_iti hutvā

(kauśikasūtra 13,22[114].4) sā tatra prāyaścittiḥ



(kauśikasūtra 13,23[115].1) atha yatra_etadgaurvāśvo vāśvataro vā puruṣo vākāśaphenamavagandhayati tatra juhuyāt

(kauśikasūtra 13,23[115].2) <payo deveṣu paya oṣadhīṣu paya āśāsu payo'ntarikṣe | tanme dhātā ca savitā ca dhattāṃ viśve taddevā abhi saṃ gṛṇantu || payo yadapsu paya usriyāsu paya utseṣūta parvateṣu | tanme dhātā ca savitā ca dhattāṃ viśve taddevā abhi saṃ gṛṇantu || yanmṛgeṣu paya āviṣṭamasti yadejati patati yatpatatriṣu [corr. Bloomfieldṅṅā 1902 514  ed. patatatriṣu] | tanme dhātā ca savitā ca dhattāṃ viśve taddevā abhi saṃ gṛṇantu || yāni payāṃsi divyārpitāni yānyantarikṣe bahudhā bahūni | teṣāmīśānaṃ vaśinī no adya pra dattā dyāvāpṛthivī ahṛṇīyamānā || [ড়ṣ1.91]> ityetena sūktena juhuyāt

(kauśikasūtra 13,23[115].3) sā tatra prāyaścittiḥ



(kauśikasūtra 13,24[116].1) atha yatra_etatpipīlikā anācārarūpā dṛśyante tatra juhuyāt

(kauśikasūtra 13,24[116].2) <bhuvāya svāhā bhuvanāya svāhā bhuvanapataye svāhā bhuvāṃ pataye svāhāvoṣāya svāhā vinatāya svāhā śatāruṇāya svāhā>

(kauśikasūtra 13,24[116].3) <yaḥ prācyāṃ diśi śvetapipīlikānāṃ rājā tasmai svāhā | yo dakṣiṇāyāṃ diśi kṛṣṇapipīlikānāṃ rājā tasmai svāhā | yaḥ pratīcyāṃ diśi rajatapipīlikānāṃ rājā tasmai svāhā | ya udīcyāṃ diśi rohitapipīlikānāṃ rājā tasmai svāhā | yo dhruvāyāṃ diśi babhrupipīlikānāṃ rājā tasmai svāhā | yo vyadhvāyāṃ diśi haritapipīlikānāṃ rājā tasmai svāhā | ya ūrdhvāyāṃ diśyaruṇapipīlikānāṃ rājā tasmai svāhā>

(kauśikasūtra 13,24[116].4) tāścedetāvatā na śāmyeyustata uttaramagnimupasamādhāya

(kauśikasūtra 13,24[116].5) śaramayaṃ barhirubhayataḥ parichinnaṃ prasavyaṃ paristīrya

(kauśikasūtra 13,24[116].6) viṣāvadhvastamiṅgiḍamājyaṃ śākapalāśena_utpūtaṃ bādhakena sruveṇa juhoti

(kauśikasūtra 13,24[116].7) <uttiṣṭhata nirdravata na va ihāstvityañcanam | indro vaḥ sarvāsāṃ sākaṃ garbhānāṇḍāni bhetsyati [ড়ṣ17.13.3]> <phaḍḍhatāḥ pipīlikāḥ [dṛṣṭavyam‌  Kauśṣ47.21]>_iti
(kauśikasūtra 13,24[116].8) <indro vo yamo vo varuṇo vo'gnirvo vāyurvaḥ sūryo vaścendro vaḥ prajāpatirva īśāno vaḥ>_iti



(kauśikasūtra 13,25[117].1) atha yatra_etat_nīlamakṣā anācārarūpā dṛśyante tatra juhuyāt

(kauśikasūtra 13,25[117].2) <yā matyaiḥ sarathaṃ yānti ghorā mṛtyordūtyaḥ kraviśaḥ saṃ babhūvuḥ |  śivaṃ cakṣuruta ghoṣaḥ śivānāṃ śaṃ no astu dvipade śaṃ catuṣpade || śāntaṃ cakṣuruta vāyasīnāṃ yā cāsāṃ ghorā manaso visṛṣṭiḥ | manasaspate tanvā mā pāhi ghorānmā vi rikṣi tanvā mā prajayā mā paśubhirvāyave svāhā []>_iti hutvā

(kauśikasūtra 13,25[117].3) <vāta ā vātu bheṣajaṃ [ড়ṣ19.46.7 ityādi]>_ityetena sūktena juhuyāt

(kauśikasūtra 13,25[117].4) <vāta ā vātu bheṣajaṃ śaṃbhu mayobhu no hṛde | pra ṇa āyūṃṣi tāriṣat || uta vāta pitāsi na uta bhrātota naḥ sakhā | sa no jīvātave kṛdhi || yadado vāta te gṛhe nihitaṃ bheṣajaṃ guhā | tasya no dehi jīvase [ড়ṣ19.46.79, Vaitṣ38.1 ityādi]>_ityetena sūktena juhuyāt

(kauśikasūtra 13,25[117].5) sā tatra prāyaścittiḥ



(kauśikasūtra 13,26[118].1) atha yatra_etat_madhumakṣikā anācārarūpā dṛśyante <madhu vātā [ed. vāta] ṛtāyate [ড়ṣ19.45.5, dṛṣṭavyam‌  Kauśṣ90.2591.1]> ityetena sūktena juhuyāt

(kauśikasūtra 13,26[118].2) sā tatra prāyaścittiḥ



(kauśikasūtra 13,27[119].1) atha yatra_etadanājñātamadbhutaṃ dṛśyate tatra juhuyāt

(kauśikasūtra 13,27[119].2) <yadajñātamanāmnātamarthasya karmaṇo mithaḥ | agne tvaṃ nastasmātpāhi sa hi vettha yathāyatham || agnaye svāhā []>

(kauśikasūtra 13,27[119].3) <vāyo sūrya candra>_iti ca

(kauśikasūtra 13,28[120].1) atha yatra_etadgrāme vāvasāne vāgniśaraṇe samajyāyāṃ vāvadīryeta catasro dhenava upakḷptā bhavanti śvetā kṛṣṇā rohiṇī surūpā caturthī

(kauśikasūtra 13,28[120].2) tāsāmetaddvādaśarātraṃ saṃdugdhaṃ navanītaṃ nidadhāti

(kauśikasūtra 13,28[120].3) dvādaśyāḥ prātaryatra_evādo_avadīrṇaṃ bhavati tata uttaramagnimupasamādhāya

(kauśikasūtra 13,28[120].5) <agnirbhūmyām [12.1.19]> iti tisṛbhirabhimantryālabhya

(kauśikasūtra 13,28[120].6) atha juhuyāt

(kauśikasūtra 13,28[120].7) tathā dakṣiṇārdhe

(kauśikasūtra 13,28[120].8) tathā paścārdhe

(kauśikasūtra 13,28[120].9) uttarārdhe saṃsthāpya vāstoṣpatyairjuhuyāt

(kauśikasūtra 13,28[120].10) avadīrṇe saṃpātānānīya saṃsthāpya homān

(kauśikasūtra 13,28[120].13) sīraṃ vaiśyo_aśvaṃ prādeśiko grāmavaraṃ rājā

(kauśikasūtra 13,28[120].14) sā tatra prāyaścittiḥ

(kauśikasūtra 13,29[121].2) sā tatra prāyaścittiḥ

(kauśikasūtra 13,30[122].3) sa yaṃ dviṣyāttasyāśāyāṃ <lohitaṃ te pra siñcāmi>_iti dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ prasiñcet



(kauśikasūtra 13,31[123].1) atha yatra_etadvapāṃ vā havīṃṣi vā vayāṃsi dvipadacatuṣpadaṃ vābhimṛśyāvagacheyur<ye agnayo [3.21.1]> <namo devavadhebhyo [6.13.1]>_ityetābhyāṃ sūktābhyāṃ juhuyāt

(kauśikasūtra 13,31[123].2) sā tatra prāyaścittiḥ



(kauśikasūtra 13,32[124].1) atha yatra_etatkumārasya kumāryā vā dvau_āvartau mūrdhanyau bhavataḥ savyāvṛdeko deśāvartastatra juhuyāt

(kauśikasūtra 13,32[124].2) <tvaṣṭā rūpāṇi bahudhā vikurvañjanayan prajā bahudhā viśvarūpāḥ | sa me karotvaviparītamasmānanupūrvaṃ kalpayatāmihaiva || tvaṣṭre svāhā []>

(kauśikasūtra 13,32[124].3) <antargarbheṣu bahudhā saṃ tanoti janayan prajā bahudhā viśvarūpāḥ | sa me karotvaviparītamasmānanupūrvaṃ kalpayatāmihaiva || tvaṣṭre svāhā []>

(kauśikasūtra 13,32[124].7) sā tatra prāyaścittiḥ

(kauśikasūtra 13,33[125].2) <yūpo virohañchataśākho adhvaraḥ samāvṛto mohayiṣyan yajamānasya lokān | vedābhigupto brahmaṇā parivṛto'tharvabhiḥ śāntaḥ sukṛtāmetu lokān || yūpo hyarukṣaddviṣatāṃ vadhāya na me yajño yajamānaśca riṣyāt | saptarṣīṇāṃ sukṛtāṃ yatra lokastatremaṃ yajñaṃ yajamānaṃ ca dhehi || vanaspate svāhā [, dṛṣṭavyam‌  Kāty̭ṣṣ2.2.8]]>_iti hutvā

(kauśikasūtra 13,33[125].3) <vanaspatiḥ saha devairna āgann [12.3.15]> iti juhuyāt

(kauśikasūtra 13,34[126].1) atha yatra_etaddivolkā patati tadayogakṣemāśaṅkaṃ bhavati_avṛṣṭyāśaṅkaṃ vā

(kauśikasūtra 13,34[126].2) tatra rājā bhūmipatirvidvāṃsaṃ brahmāṇaṃ vṛṇīyāt

(kauśikasūtra 13,34[126].3) sa vṛto_araṇyasyārdhamabhivrajya tatra dvādaśarātramanuśuṣyet

(kauśikasūtra 13,34[126].4) sa khalu pūrvaṃ navarātramāraṇyaśākamūlaphalabhakṣaścātha_uttaraṃ trirātraṃ nānyadudakāt

(kauśikasūtra 13,34[126].5) śvo bhūte sapta dhenava upakḷptā bhavanti śvetā kṛṣṇā  rohiṇī nīlā pāṭalā surūpā bahurūpā saptamī

(kauśikasūtra 13,34[126].6) tāsāmetaddvādaśarātraṃ saṃdugdhaṃ navanītaṃ nidadhāti

(kauśikasūtra 13,34[126].7) dvādaśyāḥ prātaryatra_evāsau patitā bhavati tata uttaramagnimupasamādhāya

(kauśikasūtra 13,34[126].8) parisamūhya paryukṣya paristīrya barhiḥ

(kauśikasūtra 13,43[135].9) <asau vai nāma te mātāsau vai nāma te pitā | asau vai nāma te dūtaḥ svavaṃśamadhi tiṣṭhati || uttamarātrī ṇāma mṛtyo te mātā tasya te antakaḥ pitā | samaṃdadhānaste dūtaḥ svavaṃśamadhi tiṣṭhati || bahavo'sya pāśā vitatāḥ pṛthivyāmasaṃkhyeyā aparyantā anantāḥ | yābhirvaṃśānabhinidadhāti prāṇināṃ yān kāṃścemān prāṇabhṛtāṃ jighāṃsan | sa imaṃ dūtaṃ nudatu vaṃśapṛṣṭhātsa me gachatu dviṣato niveśaṃ mṛtyave svāhā || bṛhaspatirāṅgiraso brahmaṇaḥ putro viśve devāḥ pra dadurviśvamejat | sa imaṃ dūtaṃ nudatu vaṃśapṛṣṭhātsa me gachatu dviṣato niveśaṃ bṛhaspataya āṅgirasāya svāhā || yasya te'nnaṃ na kṣīyate bhūya evopajātaye | yasmai bhūtaṃ ca bhavyaṃ ca sarvametatpratiṣṭhitam | sa imaṃ dūtaṃ nudatu vaṃśapṛṣṭhātsa me gachatu dviṣato niveśamindrāya svāhā || mukhaṃ devānāmiha yo babhūva yo jānāti vayunānāṃ samīpe | yasmai hutaṃ devatā bhakṣayanti vāyunetraḥ supraṇītiḥ sunītiḥ | sa imaṃ dūtaṃ nudatu vaṃśapṛṣṭhātsa me gachatu dviṣato niveśamagnaye svāhā || yaḥ pṛthivyāṃ cyāvayanneti vṛkṣān prabhañjanena rathena saha saṃvidānaḥ | rasān gandhān bhāvayanneti devo mātariśvā bhūtabhavyasya kartā | sa imaṃ dūtaṃ nudatu vaṃśapṛṣṭhātsa me gachatu dviṣato niveśaṃ vāyave svāhā || brahmacārī carati brahmacaryamṛcaṃ gāthāṃ brahma paraṃ jigāṃsan | taṃ vighnā anupariyanti sarve ye antarikṣe ye ca divi śritāsaḥ | taṃ viśo anuparyanti sarvāḥ karmāṇi loke
parimohayanti | sa imaṃ dūtaṃ nudatu vaṃśapṛṣṭhātsa me gachatu dviṣato niveśamādityāya svāhā || yo nakṣatraiḥ sarathaṃ yāti devaḥ saṃsiddhena rathena saha saṃvidānaḥ | rūpaṃrūpaṃ kṛṇvānaścitrabhānuḥ subhānuḥ | sa imaṃ dūtaṃ nudatu vaṃśapṛṣṭhātsa me gachatu dviṣato niveśaṃ candrāya svāhā || oṣadhayaḥ somarājñīryaśasvinīḥ | tā imaṃ dūtaṃ nudantu vaṃśapṛṣṭhātsa me gachatu dviṣato niveśamoṣadhībhyaḥ somarājñībhyaḥ svāhā || oṣadhayo varuṇarājñīryaśasvinīḥ | tā imaṃ dūtaṃ nudantu vaṃśapṛṣṭhātsa me gachatu dviṣato niveśamoṣadhībhyo varuṇarājñībhyaḥ svāhā || aṣṭasthūṇo daśapakṣo yadṛchajo vanaspate | putrāṃścaiva paśūṃścābhi rakṣa vanaspate || yo vanaspatīnāmupatāpo babhūva yadvā gṛhān ghoramutā jagāma tannirjagāma haviṣā ghṛtena śaṃ no astu dvipade śaṃ catuṣpade || yo vanaspatīnāmupatāpo na āgadyadvā yajñaṃ no'dbhutamā jagāma | sarvaṃ tadagne hutamastu bhāgaśaḥ śivān vayamuttaremābhi vājān || tvaṣṭre svāhā []>_iti hutvā
